This maneuver rapidly aligns the vehicle for corner exits, ultimately reducing lap time. Autonomously executing such maneuvers formulates a complex dual-objective control problem: stabilizing highly nonlinear drift dynamics while strictly minimizing lap time. Addressing this challenge motivates the development of advanced Minimum-Lap-Time (MLT) drift control architectures. This paper proposes a planning-control framework specifically designed for MLT drifting scenario. First, we formulate an optimal control problem to generate a MLT drift planning trajectory, which is used as prior data to train a deep reinforcement learning drift controller. Given that drifting involves extremely large sideslip angles and is therefore challenging to learn directly, a Track-guided Reinforcement Learning (TgRL) drift control method is proposed to enable progressive training in a step-by-step manner, from drift control policy, to drift corner policy, and finally to a comprehensive drift race policy. The reward function incorporates both an instant reward term and an end reward term derived from the Minimum-Lap-Time objective. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework enables the agent to learn a drift racing policy that not only ensures vehicle motion control performance but also effectively reduces lap time.

</details>


### [60] [Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators Navigating Doorways via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00206)
*Kasra Sinaei,Kasun Weerakoon,Christopher Bradley,Seyed Abolfazl Fakoorian,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非线性模型预测控制（MPC）的运动规划框架，将机器人与门建模为耦合动力学系统，在无需显式机械臂运动学建模的情况下，通过基于惩罚的约束来确保操作可行性，为移动操作机器人穿越推门和拉门生成动态可行且无碰撞的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿越门是移动操作机器人在人类环境中运行的基本能力，需要在移动基座和机械臂之间进行协调运动。这一任务要求同时考虑动态可行性、碰撞避免以及操作可行性，是一个复杂的规划问题。

Method: 将机器人与门公式化为耦合动力学系统，并嵌入到非线性MPC优化框架中。通过惩罚约束强制执行操作可行性，避免在规划器中显式建模机械臂运动学，从而降低了规划复杂度。

Result: 仿真实验和硬件实验均证明，该方法能够成功规划出穿越门的可行轨迹，适用于推门和拉门两种场景。

Conclusion: 所提出的MPC框架能有效处理移动操作机器人的门穿越任务，通过惩罚约束替代显式运动学建模的方法在保证操作可行性的同时简化了规划问题，并在仿真与实际硬件上得到验证。

Abstract: Navigating doorways is a fundamental capability for mobile manipulators operating in human environments, requiring coordinated motion between the mobile base and manipulator arm. This paper presents a motion planning framework that generates dynamically feasible and collision-free trajectories for autonomously opening and traversing both push and pull doors. The proposed method formulates the robot and door as a coupled dynamical system within a nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC) optimization framework. Manipulation feasibility is enforced through a penalty-based constraint, avoiding explicit arm kinematic modeling in the planner. Simulations and a hardware experiment demonstrate that the approach successfully plans feasible trajectories for door traversal.

</details>


### [61] [Developing Combined Manipulation and Locomotion Skills with Interaction Representation and Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00208)
*Fanxing Meng,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于发展原理的人形机器人运动策略学习方法，包括全身抓取策略学习和将抓取与站立行走策略组合的复杂操控-运动策略，实现对未见物体93%的零样本抓取成功率和96-100%的持物站立成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要能够学习复杂的操控与运动结合的技能（如抓取后站立行走），但现有方法通常依赖外部数据集或预训练模型，且难以将多个独立学习的策略有效组合到单一机器人上。

Method: (1) 受调和分析启发，采用立方调和函数作为权重，通过空间卷积表征手-物体空间关系；基于发展原理设计帧内手指关节解耦课程，使机器人无需外部数据集即可自主学习通用抓取策略。(2) 将抓取策略与独立的起身策略组合，为两者提供各自的观测向量，并通过手-物交互分数决定各策略何时控制哪些机器人关节。

Result: 未见物体的零样本抓取成功率达93%，持物站立的成功率达96-100%。同时实验表明，策略组合只有在各策略在同一完整人形身体上学习时才有效，即使某个策略（如移动）看似不需要所有身体部位（如手指）。

Conclusion: 基于发展原理的方法能够使机器人自主学习通用抓取策略并有效组合多个策略生成复杂行为，且策略学习必须在同一完整人形本体上进行组合才有效，这为多功能人形机器人技能学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper addresses how to enable a humanoid robot to learn motion policies based on developmental principles and combine policies to create more sophisticated and useful behaviors. Specifically, we present an approach to (1) learning a whole-body reaching and grasping policy and (2) combining it and a standing-up and walking policy to compose a more complex policy of manipulation and locomotion: grasping, standing up, and walking.
  In (1), our method draws inspiration from harmonic analysis and adopts cubic harmonics as weights to represent the hand-object spatial relationship via spatial convolution. Utilizing an intra-episode finger joint decoupling curriculum based on developmental principles, a robot can autonomously learn a generalizable grasping policy without relying on external datasets or pretrained models.
  In (2), our method combines the grasping policy with a separately learned getting-up policy by providing both policies with their respective observation vectors and using hand-object interaction scores to determine when each policy should control which robot joints. Our results show a 93% zero-shot success rate for grasping unseen objects and a 96-100% success rate for standing up while holding the object. Our work also demonstrates that combining different policies is only effective if each policy learning happens on the same whole humanoid body even if a policy (such as for locomotion) does not seem to need all the body parts (such as fingers).

</details>


### [62] [Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact: Distributed Contact Modeling for Sphere-Based Robot Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00263)
*Nataliya Nechyporenko,Ava Abderezaei,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CSLC的分布式接触模型，在球体机器人表示基础上通过柔性晶格结构实现接触补丁的物理精确建模，兼顾计算效率与物理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有球体机器人表示虽然计算高效（快速碰撞检测和可微几何），但仅依赖点接触假设，无法捕捉接触补丁面积、压力分布、旋转刚度和摩擦力矩等关键物理特性，限制了物理精度。

Method: 将机器人界面建模为通过锚定弹簧和横向弹簧连接的表面球体柔性晶格。当与物体接触时，晶格产生变形，形成空间分布的接触补丁，从而在球体表示的基础上提升物理精度。

Result: 在两个独立求解器上验证了CSLC模型，初步结果展示了接触补丁的形成以及对抓取稳定性的改善。

Conclusion: CSLC模型在保持球体表示计算优势的同时，通过分布式接触建模显著提升了物理真实性，为机器人接触规划提供了一条兼顾效率与精度的新途径。

Abstract: Contact planning in robotics requires models that are both computationally efficient and physically accurate. Sphere-based robot representations satisfy the first requirement by enabling fast collision checking and differentiable geometry, but sacrifice physical accuracy by relying on point contact which cannot capture contact patch area, pressure distributions, rotational stiffness, or frictional moments. We introduce Compliant Sphere Lattice Contact (CSLC), a distributed contact model that operates natively on sphere representations by modeling the robot interface as a compliant lattice of surface spheres connected through anchor and lateral springs. When pressed against an object, the lattice deforms to produce a spatially distributed contact patch that improves the physical accuracy of sphere-based contact. We validate CSLC across two independent solvers and show preliminary results demonstrating contact patch formation and improved grasp stability.

</details>


### [63] [Localization in Spatiotemporal Fields via Environmental PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00272)
*Jose Fuentes,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Ana Cavalcanti,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏微分方程（PDE）控制的时空场作为定位签名的定位框架，利用浅水方程和对流扩散方程生成预测场，并采用Rao-Blackwellized粒子滤波（RBPF）融合多模态测量进行定位，仿真与实船实验均验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定位方法依赖GPS或预先布置的信标，在近海、河流等环境中可能受限或失效。本文利用环境中天然存在的物理场（如水流、温度、盐度、溶解氧等）作为定位信号，克服了对人工基础设施的依赖，但环境场具有时空变化特性，需要有效的建模与估计方法。

Method: 采用两类PDE（浅水方程描述自由表面流动，对流扩散方程描述标量输运）通过数值求解器生成预测环境场；将多个场通道作为多模态测量融合。在RBPF框架下，将车辆状态分解为非线性部分（由粒子采样）和线性传感器偏置部分（由每粒子的卡尔曼滤波解析追踪），从而减少所需粒子数并处理传感器漂移。

Result: 仿真实验在两种PDE场景下表明，RBPF在最终位置误差和RMSE上均优于标准粒子滤波，且在不同粒子数下保持一致优势。实船实验以自主水面艇测量盐度、温度和溶解氧，验证了PDE控制的环境场具有足够空间变异性以支持实际定位。

Conclusion: PDE控制的环境场可作为有效的定位签名，RBPF框架通过状态分解降低了粒子需求并处理了传感器漂移，在仿真和实际实验中均展现出优于标准粒子滤波的性能，为无GPS环境下的自主定位提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a localization framework that uses spatiotemporal fields governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) as localization signatures. Two PDE classes are considered: the shallow water equations, which describe free-surface flows in coastal and riverine environments, and the advection-diffusion equation, which models the transport and mixing of scalar quantities such as temperature, salinity, and dissolved oxygen. A numerical PDE solver provides predicted fields over the domain, and multiple field channels are fused as multimodal measurements to improve localization accuracy. We formulate the problem within a Rao-Blackwellized particle filter (RBPF) that partitions the vehicle state into a nonlinear component sampled by particles and a linear sensor bias component tracked analytically via per-particle Kalman filters. This factorization reduces the required number of particles compared to a standard particle filter while accounting for realistic sensor drift. Simulation studies on both PDE scenarios show that the RBPF consistently outperforms a standard particle filter in terms of final position error and Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) across varying particle counts. Field experiments with an autonomous surface vehicle measuring salinity, temperature, and dissolved oxygen validate that PDE-governed environmental fields provide sufficient spatial variability for practical localization. Related experimental videos are available at https://localization-environmental-pdes.github.io/.

</details>


### [64] [Hybrid Attention Estimation Pipeline for Adaptive HRI Using an Expressive Robotic Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00284)
*Pablo Moraes,Monica Rodriguez,Christopher Peters,Hiago Sodre,Tobias Doernbach,Bruna Guterres,Ricardo Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合视觉注意力估计方法，用于基于InMoov生态系统的表情机器人头部的人机交互。系统结合快速几何感知层与基于视觉语言模型的语义感知层，通过有限状态机整合信号以调节自适应交互行为，并在10名参与者的40次试验中验证了其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，机器人需要实时感知用户的注意力状态以实现自然的交互行为调节。然而，单一的几何感知方法（如人脸检测和头部姿态估计）难以提供丰富的语义上下文信息，而纯语义方法又难以满足高频交互的时间调节需求。因此，需要一种结合两种互补感知能力的混合方法。

Method: 提出一个双感知层的流水线：(1) 几何感知层提供高频率的头部和面部姿态信息，用于时间调节；(2) 语义感知层仅接收原始第一视角摄像头帧，通过视觉语言模型生成上下文化的注意力标签（如注意力朝向机器人、使用手机或注意力分散到其他处）。两个层的输出通过有限状态机整合，以此调节自适应交互行为（包括激活、等待、交互恢复和返回静止状态）。

Result: 实验采用10名参与者和40次试验，涵盖基线与自适应交互条件。结果显示：所有试验中交互启动可靠；自适应分心条件下暂停行为一致；几何层与语义层的输出之间存在非冗余的互补语义信息。

Conclusion: 该项应用案例研究表明，混合视觉注意力估计方法能够有效结合几何感知与语义感知的优势，实现可靠且自适应的机器人交互行为调节，且两个感知层提供的信息具有互补性，证明了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an applied case study on hybrid visual attention estimation for human-robot interaction using an expressive robotic head based on the InMoov ecosystem. The proposed pipeline combines a fast geometric perception layer with an independent semantic perception layer based on a vision-language model. The geometric layer provides high-frequency face and head-pose information for temporal regulation, while the semantic layer receives only raw egocentric camera frames and produces contextual attention labels related to attention toward the robot, phone use, or attention elsewhere. These signals are integrated through a finite state machine that regulates adaptive interaction behavior, including activation, waiting, interaction resumption, and return to rest. The system was evaluated with 10 participants across 40 trials covering baseline and adaptive interaction conditions. Results show reliable interaction start across all trials, consistent pause behavior in the adaptive distraction condition, and non-redundant semantic information between the geometric and semantic outputs.

</details>


### [65] [Towards General Language-Conditioned Latent Safety Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00315)
*Ihab Tabbara,Yuxuan Yang,Hussein Sibai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语言条件的安全过滤器，将Hamilton-Jacobi安全actor和critic以自然语言指定的约束为条件，使机器人策略能够适应不同用户、环境和应用场景下变化的安全要求，而不必重新设计或重新学习安全过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全过滤器大多针对特定约束设计，当安全要求变化时需要重新设计或重新学习，无法适应越来越通用的VLA机器人策略在不同场景下的多样化安全需求。

Method: 将Hamilton-Jacobi安全actor和critic以语言条件化，使其能根据自然语言描述的安全约束动态调整安全过滤行为，并在视觉环境中进行评估。

Result: 在pick-and-place、擦桌和叠积木三类任务中，语言条件化的安全过滤器能够有效减少约束违反，并且对未见过的约束实例展现出部分迁移能力。

Conclusion: 语言条件化的安全过滤为通用机器人策略的安全部署提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，能够适应变化的安全要求，并在约束泛化方面展现出初步能力。

Abstract: Robot policies are becoming increasingly general, with vision-language-action (VLA) models enabling a single policy to execute diverse tasks specified in natural language. Safe deployment, however, requires adapting not only to new tasks but also to varying safety requirements across users, environments, and applications. Existing safety filters remain largely constraint-specific and thus must be redesigned or relearned when safety requirements change. In this paper, we investigate language-conditioned safety filtering, in which a Hamilton-Jacobi safety actor and critic are conditioned on language-specified constraints. We evaluate this formulation across pick-and-place, table-wiping, and block-stacking tasks in the vision-based setting, examining its ability to enforce language-specified constraints and transfer to unseen constraint instances within the evaluated constraint families. Our experiments provide evidence that language-conditioned safety filters reduce constraint violations and exhibit partial transfer to unseen constraint instances.

</details>


### [66] [Belief-Space Perception Routing under Coupled Sensor Faults and Compute Contention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00322)
*Sparsh Roy,Vihan Aggarwal,Davin Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种感知路由器，通过联合跟踪传感器故障状态和计算争用状态的概率估计（以噪声OR项耦合），动态选择四种YOLO检测器配置之一以确保帧在截止时间前完成。在两者并发场景下，相比独立处理策略，截止时间错失率降低1.1~9.4个百分点（六个条件中五个排除零，符号检验p=0.001）。但进一步在真实恶劣天气序列中发现传感器故障与计算争用之间的耦合并非自然出现（24项检验均不显著），因此路由结果被限定为机制验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人依赖固定时钟进行视觉感知时面临两个并发问题：相机在雨、泥、雾、黑暗中性能退化；板载单处理器需与规划和控制共享算力，导致感知可用的计算资源逐秒波动。现有系统大多将这两个问题分开建模，缺乏联合处理方案。

Method: 提出感知路由器：同时跟踪传感器故障状态与计算争用状态的概率估计，通过噪声OR项耦合两者，利用耦合后的估计从四种检测器配置（YOLO11x/n在1280或640像素分辨率）中选择一种，使帧处理在截止时间前完成。路由器成本仅数十微秒/帧。随后通过RADIATE恶劣天气数据集（8个序列）和3种独立于故障信号的工作量代理进行24项检验（经Benjamini-Hochberg校正并重复运行），验证耦合是否自然存在。

Result: 耦合策略在传感器故障与计算争用并发时，截止时间错失率相比独立策略降低1.1~9.4个百分点；六个条件中五个置信区间排除零，10个序列6个条件下的合并效应符号检验p=0.001；所有非耦合对照和故障无关轨迹均为0.0个百分点。但8个真实恶劣天气序列中24项检验均未发现故障状态与计算争用之间的自然耦合，故将路由结果限定为机制验证。

Conclusion: 联合感知路由在人为构造的耦合条件下能有效降低截止时间错失率，证明该机制可行；然而在真实恶劣天气数据中未观察到传感器故障与计算争用之间的自然耦合。野外场景是否真正存在此类耦合仍待进一步验证，作者发布了评估管线供部署方在自身轨迹上验证。

Abstract: A robot that has to see and react on a fixed clock runs into two problems at once. Its cameras degrade in rain, mud, fog, and darkness. And the single onboard processor it runs on is shared with planning and control, so the compute left over for perception moves around from second to second. Most systems model the two separately. We present a perception router that tracks probabilistic estimates of sensor-fault state and compute- contention state, couples them with a noisy-OR term, and uses the coupled estimate to pick one of four detector configurations (YOLO11x/n at 1280 or 640 px) so that the frame finishes before its deadline. Where the two stressors co-occur, the coupled policy cuts the deadline-miss rate by 1.1 to 9.4 percentage points against a policy that treats them independently. The interval excludes zero in five of six conditions, the pooled effect over 10 sequences and 6 conditions has sign-test p = 0.001, and every uncoupled control and the fault-free trajectory sit at exactly 0.0 pp. Routing costs tens of microseconds per frame. We then asked whether the coupling the method exploits arises on its own. Across eight real RADIATE adverse-weather sequences and three workload proxies independent of the fault signal, after Benjamini-Hochberg correction and a replication run, none of 24 tests found it. We report that null and scope the routing result as a proof of mechanism. Whether such coupling occurs in the field is still open, and the released evaluation pipeline lets a deployment settle it on its own traces.

</details>


### [67] [Action Chunk Scheduling for Batched Robot Policy Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00337)
*Rohan Bansal,David He,Nadun Ranawaka Arachchige,Zhenyang Chen,Soobum Kim,Kexin Rong,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了机器人基础模型远程部署的调度问题，并构建了Armory服务系统，在模拟和真实机器人车队上验证，提出考虑动作块异质性的调度算法，在真实实验中吞吐量提升最高18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型（如VLA）计算需求大，而端侧设备在功耗和空间上受限，难以在本地部署。需要探索从远程GPU向多台机器人提供服务机器人策略的方式，以实现规模化部署。

Method: 将"从远程GPU向多机器人提供策略服务"形式化为调度问题，构建Armory服务系统，并在模拟与真实机器人车队的场景中验证。提出一种考虑机器人在不同动作块消耗速率差异的调度算法，以解决传统批处理方法与机器人策略执行闭环需求之间的不匹配。

Result: 实验表明，当所有机器人同质时，朴素调度启发式表现良好；但当机器人以不同速率消耗动作块时，传统方法表现不佳。所提出的调度算法在真实实验中实现系统整体吞吐量最高18%的提升。

Conclusion: 机器人策略执行存在闭环实时性要求，传统批处理调度方法无法直接套用，需要针对动作块消耗速率的异质性设计专门的调度算法，从而在远程GPU服务场景下显著提升系统吞吐量。

Abstract: Deploying robot foundation models at scale is the next step towards realizing the potential of general-purpose robots. However, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and other foundation models are computationally demanding, and on-device compute is constrained by power and space. In this paper, we introduce the problem of serving a robot policy to multiple robots from a remote GPU and formulate it as a scheduling problem. We build Armory, a serving system validated on fleets of both simulated and real robots. Our experiments show that naive scheduling heuristics perform well when all robots are the same, but fall short when robots consume action chunks at different rates, uncovering a mismatch between conventional batching methods and the closed-loop requirements of robot policy execution. To address this, we propose a scheduling algorithm that accounts for this heterogeneity and improves overall system throughput by up to $18\%$ in real-world experiments. Additional details are available at https://gatech-rl2.github.io/actionchunkscheduling.

</details>


### [68] [A Robotic System for Automated Manufacturing of Dielectric Elastomer Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00369)
*Van Remenar,Anatol Gogoj,Ang Li,Tomas Verardi,Matei Mandoiu,Victor Jimenez Santiago,Mihai Duduta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套自动化机器人制造系统，用于生产兼作执行器和传感器的软电容器。系统重点优化了决定器件质量的两个关键工艺：旋涂法介电层成型和模压法碳纳米管(CNT)电极涂覆。共制造了20个多层介电弹性体执行器(DEAs)，每个含12个平均厚度55.37±2.04微米的介电层和11个交替排列的CNT电极，总工艺时间减少14.2%，并将操作员参与时间降低56.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软电容器作为执行器和传感器在软体机器人等领域具有重要应用价值，但其制造过程高度依赖人工操作，导致效率低、一致性差、质量控制困难。因此需要开发自动化制造系统以提升工艺可重复性、缩短生产时间并减少人为干预。

Method: 构建自动化机器人制造系统，聚焦两个对器件质量起决定性作用的工艺环节：1) 采用旋涂(spin coating)工艺形成介电层；2) 采用模压(stamping)工艺涂覆CNT电极。通过机器人集成实现全流程自动化，并制造多层叠层结构的DEA进行验证。

Result: 成功制造20个多层DEAs，每个器件包含12个介电层（平均厚度55.37±2.04微米）和11个交替CNT电极。与人工制造相比，总工艺时间缩短14.2%，操作员参与时间减少56.1%，同时保持了良好的厚度一致性（标准差约2微米）。

Conclusion: 自动化机器人制造系统能够有效提升软电容器的生产效率和一致性，显著降低对人工操作的时间和依赖，为软电子器件的规模化、可重复化制造提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This letter presents an automated robotic manufacturing system for soft capacitors which operate as actuators and sensors. Emphasis is placed on the two processes that most directly govern device quality, dielectric layer formation by spin coating and carbon nanotube (CNT) electrode application by stamping. Twenty multilayer DEAs, each comprising 12 dielectric layers with a mean thickness of 55.37 +- 2.04 um and 11 alternating CNT electrodes, were fabricated reducing total process time by 14.2% and removing the operator from 56.1% of it.

</details>


### [69] [The Gate, Not the Cache: Gate Provenance Bounds the Closed-Loop Reliability of Training-Free VLA Token Skipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00391)
*Qi Luo,Shuaijun Liu,Hao Zhao,Kunlin Li,Xiaobo Wang,Ningxing Su,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的token跳过加速方法，发现当门控信号来自模型自身加速前向传播时，token跳过会导致闭环控制性能崩溃（skip ratio 0.9时从1.00跌至0.31-0.68），且该崩溃对动作级检测器不可见。作者提出"执行间隙刷新"（actuation-slack refresh），在机器人执行动作块期间于关键路径之外执行一次密集前向传播，为下一步提供干净的门控和新的KV缓存，使两种跳过机制均恢复至0.98，延迟比密集模型降低18-22%。结论是：门控信号的来源（而非跳过机制本身）决定了加速VLA的闭环可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练无需的token跳过被广泛用于加速VLA模型，但当门控从自身加速前向传播中获取时，被跳过的token对下一步门控的可见性最低，误差会跨控制步骤累积导致任务失败。作者旨在揭示这一崩溃的机制根源，并设计低成本修复方案。

Method: 作者系统对比了两种token跳过机制（复用reuse与删除deletion）分别配合两种门控来源（原始密集前向与加速前向）在相同episode上的表现。基于门控干净性决定成败的发现，提出actuation-slack refresh：利用机器人执行动作块的间隙，在关键路径外运行一次密集前向传播，产出干净门控和新鲜KV缓存，无条件（而非触发式）应用于下一步。并将该刷新集成到现有的caching和pruning方法中。

Result: 在LIBERO-Object上skip ratio为0.9时，自收获门控下复用机制崩溃至0.68、删除机制崩溃至0.31（密集基线为1.00），且动作级检测器无法察觉该崩溃。采用refresh后两种机制均恢复至0.98。在2个VLA策略、4个LIBERO套件和4个SIMPLER任务上，refresh修复了所有由自收获门控导致的崩溃，服务延迟比密集模型降低18-22%（仿真和实体机器人均验证）。

Conclusion: 加速VLA的闭环可靠性由门控信号的来源决定，而非token被跳过的方式。自收获门控会导致错误跨步骤累积，而关键路径外的密集刷新以很小的开销同时保留了跳过加速的速度优势和密集前向的信息完整性。

Abstract: Token skipping is a widely used training-free way to accelerate vision--language--action (VLA) models by bypassing computation for most visual tokens at each control step according to a gate. When the next gate is harvested from the previous accelerated forward, however, the tokens skipped at one step are also the ones least visible to the next gate, and the damage can compound across control steps until the task fails. We study the two mechanisms this class is built on, reuse and deletion, crossing each against where its gate signal comes from on identical episodes. At a skip ratio of 0.9 on LIBERO-Object, both collapse when the gate comes from the model's own accelerated forwards, to 0.68 under reuse and to 0.31 under deletion against a dense 1.00, and the collapse is invisible to the action-level detectors we evaluate. What separates collapse from dense-level operation is not the mechanism but whether the gate is clean, computed by a forward that skipped nothing. We therefore propose actuation-slack refresh, one dense pass run while the robot executes its current action chunk, off the critical path, that hands the next step a clean gate and a fresh KV base. Since the measured detectors do not reliably reveal the failure, the refresh is unconditional rather than triggered. Both mechanisms then recover to 0.98, keeping the speed of skipping and the information of a dense pass. We then integrate the refresh into state-of-the-art caching and pruning methods across two VLA policies, 4 LIBERO suites, and 4 SIMPLER tasks, where it repairs every collapse caused by using a self-harvested gate. Serve latency drops 18--22\% below dense, measured both in simulation and on a physical robot. Where the gate signal comes from, not how tokens are skipped, decides closed-loop reliability for accelerated VLAs.

</details>


### [70] [Embodied Passive Aeroacoustic Perception Enables Relative Sensing and Pursuit Between Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00401)
*Yanbaihui Liu,Ravi Prakash,Li-Yu Lo,Nils Roede,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SonicFly的被动气动声学感知框架，使无人机仅通过跟随者自身飞行时产生的自然声音来估计并追踪另一架无人机，无需主动声学信号、机间通信、GPS或外部感知设施。实验结果在多样化户外轨迹中实现了1.34米的平均距离保持误差，证明了气动声学信号足以支持相对感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行机器人会产生结构化的气动声场，但这些信号在随航相对感知方面尚未被充分探索，尤其是在强自噪声干扰下。作者试图挖掘飞行自然产生的声音作为机载相对感知的信息来源，从而突破传统感知对通信、GPS或外部基础设施的依赖。

Method: SonicFly框架包含轻量级四麦克风阵列、旋翼飞行器声学表征、神经方位-距离估计器和置信度门控滤波模块，用于实现闭环飞行追踪。系统完全依赖领航无人机的固有飞行声音进行感知与跟随。

Result: 通过声学表征、机载定位和户外追踪实验，验证了多旋翼气动声学信号即使在高自噪声干扰、环境变化和飞行几何动态改变的条件下仍包含足够的相对感知信息。纯声学追踪的平均距离保持误差为1.34米。声学通道分析揭示了谐波结构、频谱可分离性和空间声学线索在可观测性中的设计原理。

Conclusion: 研究建立了飞行机器人被动气动声学感知的可行性，表明自然产生的行为信号可以作为机器人感知与协同的信息来源，提出了指导该类感知系统设计的原则。

Abstract: Aerial robots generate structured aeroacoustic fields during flight, yet these signals have been underexplored as a source of onboard relative perception, particularly under the strong ego-acoustic interference generated during simultaneous flight in various outdoor conditions. We introduce embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, a sensing paradigm in which an aerial robot infers actionable relative-state information from the naturally generated sound of flight while operating within its own evolving aeroacoustic field. We present SonicFly, a passive aeroacoustic perception framework that enables one unmanned aerial vehicle to estimate and follow another using only the leader's intrinsic flight sound, without active acoustic signaling, inter-robot communication, GPS sharing, or external sensing infrastructure. The system uses a lightweight four-microphone array, rotorcraft-informed acoustic representations, a neural bearing-range estimator, and confidence-gated filtering for closed-loop flight. Through acoustic characterization, onboard localization, and outdoor pursuit experiments, we show that multirotor aeroacoustic signals contain sufficient information to support relative perception despite strong ego-acoustic interference, environmental variability, and changing flight geometry. During acoustic-only pursuit, SonicFly achieved a mean distance-maintenance error of 1.34 m across diverse outdoor trajectories and operating conditions. Analysis of the acoustic channel reveals design principles governing embodied passive aeroacoustic perception, including the roles of harmonic structure, spectral separability, and spatial acoustic cues in determining observability. Our results establish the feasibility of embodied passive aeroacoustic perception for aerial robots and suggest that naturally generated behavioral signals can serve as information for robotic perception and coordination.

</details>


### [71] [Learning to Predict Contact Force Distributions from Vision Leveraging Object Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00464)
*Ryo Hanai,Yukiyasu Domaea,Ixchel G. Ramirez-Alpizar,Abdullah Mustafa,Floris Erich,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文旨在赋予机器人基于视觉和先验经验进行物理预测的能力。作者提出预测平滑的三维力分布而非原始点力，通过统计平滑和几何引导平滑处理刚体仿真器输出的点力，构建了从单张RGB图像预测堆积日常物品力分布的模型，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出优越的预测准确性和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够基于视觉和先验经验做出粗略的物理预测并调整操纵策略，而现有仿真器输出的噪点式点力与人类一致的预测能力存在差距。作者推测预测平滑的力分布而非原始点力可以同时改善力预测本身和下游任务性能。

Method: 使用刚体仿真器收集视觉-力配对数据；对仿真器输出的点力应用统计平滑生成目标力分布；在平滑过程中引入物体几何信息以考虑接触状态的变化；构建从单张RGB图像预测三维力分布的神经网络模型。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中进行了广泛评估。结果表明：该方法提升了预测准确性，平滑改善了下游任务性能，几何引导的平滑进一步带来增益。尽管仅在仿真中训练，模型能够有效泛化到真实场景。

Conclusion: 预测平滑力分布优于预测原始点力，且几何引导的平滑能够实现更一致的视觉预测，仿真到真实的泛化能力验证了该方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Based on vision and prior experience, humans can make rough physical predictions and adjust their manipulation strategies. This paper aims to endow robots with a similar ability. To collect paired data of vision and forces, we use a rigid-body simulator commonly adopted in robotics. However, unlike simulators that output noisy point forces, humans are able to make consistent predictions even in unfamiliar situations. Based on this observation, we hypothesize that predicting smooth force distributions rather than raw point forces can improve both force prediction itself and downstream task performance. To validate this hypothesis, we construct a model that predicts three-dimensional force distributions from a single RGB image of piled daily objects. The target distribution is generated by applying statistical smoothing to point forces obtained from the simulator. Moreover, by incorporating object geometry into the smoothing process, we aim to account for variations in contact states and achieve more consistent vision-based predictions. We conduct extensive evaluations in both simulation and real environments. Results show that our approach improves prediction accuracy, enhances downstream task performance through smoothing, and further benefits from geometry-guided smoothing. Remarkably, the trained model generalizes effectively to real-world scenes despite being trained solely in simulation.

</details>


### [72] [From Digital to Physical Reservoir Computing: Co-Optimizing Soft Robotic Reservoirs via Dynamics Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00484)
*Nicola Visentin,Maximilian Stölzle,Mariano Ramírez Montero,Francesco Braghin,Daniela Rus,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对软体机器人物理储层计算的动力学级协同优化方法，通过联合优化物理参数、微分同胚状态映射和前馈-反馈控制，使物理储层能够预训练以匹配高性能数字参考动力学。在分类和预测任务上的平均相对改进达到33.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人物理储层计算（PRC）具有天然的柔顺非线性动力学优势，但现有物理储层通常是"即用型"（adopted as-is），未经预训练或协同优化，其性能相比数字储层可能受限。作者希望探索物理储层能否像数字储层一样通过预训练来提升性能。

Method: 提出一种联合优化框架：同时优化物理参数、微分同胚物理-参考状态映射（diffeomorphic physical-reference state map）以及前馈-反馈控制。采用可微分的物理模型和加速度级方程误差（acceleration-level equation-error）目标函数，避免时间积分带来的数值问题。具体实现以模拟软体机器人为主体，以随机振荡器网络（RON）为参考动力学，使用并行多起点梯度下降进行优化。

Result: 在四个储层维度上，对分类任务（sMNIST和ADIAC）和预测任务（Mackey-Glass和Lorenz96）进行评估。优化后的软体机器人储层相比未优化的软体机器人储层，在所有任务和数据集上实现了33.7%的平均相对改进，且性能接近数字参考储层。

Conclusion: 结果表明，所提出的动力学级协同优化方法对文中考察的模拟软体机器人储层是可行的，证实了物理储层可以通过预训练对标数字参考动力学来显著提升性能。

Abstract: Soft robotic substrates are promising for Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) because their compliant nonlinear dynamics can provide temporal memory, high-dimensional state transformations, and efficient inference. However, physical reservoirs are often adopted as-is rather than pretrained or co-optimized, potentially limiting soft robotic PRC performance relative to digital reservoirs. We investigate whether a physical reservoir can instead be pretrained against high-performing digital reference dynamics. Our formulation jointly optimizes physical parameters, a diffeomorphic physical-reference state map, and feedforward-feedback control using a differentiable physical model and an acceleration-level equation-error objective that avoids temporal integration. As a proof of concept, we instantiate the formulation with simulated soft robots, a Random Oscillators Network (RON) reference, and parallel multi-start gradient descent. We evaluate the optimized reservoirs on classification (sMNIST and ADIAC) and forecasting (Mackey-Glass and Lorenz96) tasks across four reservoir dimensions. Compared with unoptimized soft robot reservoirs, the optimized reservoirs achieve a mean relative improvement of 33.7% across all tasks and datasets, while remaining close to the digital reference. These results demonstrate the feasibility of dynamics-level co-optimization for the simulated soft robotic reservoirs considered here.

</details>


### [73] [First Deployable Dynamic-CoM: A Unified Policy and Method-Agnostic Benchmark for Humanoid Single-Leg Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00500)
*Yikai Zhou,Xingyun Wang,Jieming Cui,Bozhou Chen,Yikai Fan,Yixin Zhu,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FDDC（首个可部署的动态质心观测）方法，解决了人形机器人单腿平衡问题。通过将捕获点（xCoM）分解为可从编码器和IMU重建的观测，并结合逐项翻译自人类姿势控制的奖励库，在86/90个动作上实现干净单腿平衡，并成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人。同时发布了首个方法无关、可复现的单腿平衡sim2sim基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一人形机器人大规模策略虽然能处理敏捷全身运动，但在简单的单腿站立任务上表现不佳——8个SOTA通用策略在90个测试动作中全部无法保持干净单腿站立，只能通过迈步或跳跃来恢复平衡而非预防失衡。根本原因是预防失衡需要捕获点（xCoM），即质心（CoM）及其速度的外推，但该观测需要机体线速度，而没有任何机载传感器能够直接提供，导致动态CoM观测从未被用于硬件策略。

Method: 1）关键洞察：将CoM速度相对于支撑脚进行表达时，机体线速度会精确抵消，从而得到一个仅需编码器和IMU即可重建的无速度观测。2）将该动态CoM观测直接放入硬件上运行的actor网络中。3）构建逐项翻译自人类姿势控制的奖励库，遵循"预防优于修复"的原则。4）使用非对称FastSAC训练，配备特权critic，无需蒸馏。5）发布方法无关、可复现的sim2sim基准，在训练模拟器不同的另一模拟器中评测每个策略。

Result: FDDC在90个留出动作中（覆盖9个分层姿势类别）成功保持86个干净单腿平衡，并成功迁移到真实Unitree G1机器人。消融实验表明，动态CoM观测是最大的性能驱动因素：仅移除该观测就导致干净单腿平衡分数下降40分。

Conclusion: FDDC证明动态CoM观测可以从已有传感器重建并直接驱动硬件策略，将人形机器人单腿平衡从"任务特定的技巧"转化为"可测量的通用能力"。发布的sim2sim基准为领域提供了标准化、可复现的评测方式，推动平衡能力从工程个案走向可度量的学科基础。

Abstract: Unified humanoid policies handle agile whole-body motion, yet stumble on a simple demand: staying balanced on one leg. On our single-leg-balance benchmark, eight released state-of-the-art general policies hold a clean single-leg stance on 0 of 90 test motions; they stay up only by stepping or hopping, recovering from imbalance rather than preventing it. Prevention needs the capture point (xCoM), the center of mass (CoM) extrapolated by its velocity, which has never driven a hardware policy because it requires a base linear velocity no on-board sensor provides; expressed relative to the support foot, that velocity cancels exactly, leaving an observation reconstructible from encoders and IMU alone. We put this first deployable dynamic-CoM observation directly into the actor that runs on hardware, and pair it with a reward library translated term by term from human postural control, under one principle: prevention over repair. Trained by asymmetric FastSAC with a privileged critic and no distillation, the resulting policy, FDDC (First Deployable Dynamic-CoM), holds clean single-leg balance on 86 of 90 held-out motions across nine stratified pose classes and transfers to a real Unitree G1; in ablation, the dynamic-CoM observation is the single largest driver: removing it alone costs 40 points of clean single-leg balance. We release the full stack with the first method-agnostic, reproducible sim2sim benchmark for humanoid single-leg balance, scoring each policy in a simulator distinct from its training one, a step toward turning balance from a per-task trick into a capability the field can measure.

</details>


### [74] [SSTG-Nav: Metric-Grounded Spatial-Semantic Topological Graphs for Reusable Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00527)
*Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SSTG-Nav，一种可复用的度量-语义记忆系统，将一次性场景预探索转化为可执行的物体目标，解决了服务机器人在长期部署中重复语义导航的挑战。在HM3D-v2数据集上，该系统的几何成功上限达99.4%，语义任务SR/SPL从0.835/0.560提升至0.920/0.603，Top-3恢复策略使Success@1/2/3达到0.928/0.965/0.975，并展示了完整的ROS2/Nav2落地实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ObjectNav（物体导航）任务被主导地表述为一次性探索问题，忽略了机器人长期部署的核心挑战：即使识别出物体，也无法确定可达的停止位置；且一个地图错误就可能导致任务失败。服务机器人在数月内回到同一空间时，应该利用经验而非从零搜索。

Method: SSTG-Nav包含三个核心组件：(1) 目标无关的拓扑结构，将一次性场景巡查转化为可操作的物体目标；(2) 跨视角的证据聚合，通过度量语义记忆巩固识别结果；(3) 空间可区分的恢复待机点，用于融合感知的Top-3恢复策略。此外构建了完整的查询到执行管道，并在ROS2/Nav2中实现。

Result: 在1,000个HM3D-v2轨迹、36个场景上：目标无关拓扑实现99.4%的几何成功上限；固定语义响应下，度量接地将SR/SPL从0.835/0.560提升至0.920/0.603；源感知融合达到0.926/0.586；Top-3恢复使Success@1/2/3达0.928/0.965/0.975，SPL@3达0.601。控制实验（模型、视场、密度、腐败）揭示了增益来源。

Conclusion: 预探索（pre-exploration）被确立为一种强大的实用范式，能够支撑可靠且可重复的语义导航。SSTG-Nav证明了记忆复用、证据融合和多候选恢复策略可显著提升长期部署中服务机器人的导航可靠性。

Abstract: Service robots operating for months in the same homes, offices, and facilities should become more reliable with experience instead of searching familiar space from scratch for every request. Yet ObjectNav is predominantly formulated as one-shot exploration, leaving a central deployment challenge unresolved: recognizing an object does not identify a reachable place to stop, and one confident map error can terminate the task. We introduce SSTG-Nav, a reusable metric-semantic memory that turns a one-time survey into actionable object goals, consolidates evidence across viewpoints, and retains spatially distinct recovery standoffs. On 1,000 HM3D-v2 episodes across 36 scenes, our goal-independent topology achieves a 99.4% geometric success ceiling. Holding semantic responses fixed, metric grounding raises SR/SPL from 0.835/0.560 to 0.920/0.603, and source-aware fusion reaches 0.926/0.586. Fusion-aware Top-3 recovery raises Success@1/2/3 to 0.928/0.965/0.975 and reaches 0.601 SPL@3. Model, field-of-view, density, and corruption controls identify where these gains originate, and a ROS2/Nav2 realization demonstrates the complete reusable query-to-execution pipeline. Together, the results establish pre-exploration as a powerful practical regime for dependable, repeated semantic navigation.

</details>


### [75] [Disentangling Visuo-Tactile Foresight: Oracle-Guided Interface Discovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00547)
*Zihang Yao,Chaoyue Ding,Yingying Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF)框架，通过使用仿真中成功轨迹提供的配对RGB和触觉未来信号，独立研究"未来到动作"接口。在此框架下提出Asymmetric Phase-Local Future Memory (AFM)，实现视觉与触觉未来信号的相位对齐选择性路由。在UniVTAC仿真基准的七个任务上，AFM达到32.0%平均成功率，优于模态隔离方法IFM的23.7%和基线UniVTAC-ACT的14.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操纵任务依赖物理交互线索，而这些线索从纯视觉中难以观测。近期触觉世界动作模型联合预测未来视觉观测和触觉信号以指导动作生成，但未来信号应如何结构化以有效服务于动作专家这一问题尚未被充分探索。端到端学习世界动作模型难以直接研究此问题，因为其纠缠了物理无效的视觉未来、不可靠的预测、不准确的跨模态不一致触觉预测以及不可读的未来到动作接口。

Method: 提出OVTF受控框架，使用仿真中成功轨迹的GT配对RGB和触觉未来信号来提供未来，从而固定未来提供者、隔离接口研究。在此框架内提出AFM架构：视觉记忆读取未来视觉，每个触觉记忆联合关注自身触觉流与相位对齐的未来视觉，同时阻断跨触觉访问。与IFM（模态隔离，移除视觉到触觉访问）进行对比实验。

Result: 在UniVTAC仿真基准的七个任务上，AFM平均成功率为32.0%，IFM为23.7%，UniVTAC-ACT基线为14.9%。结果显示选择性相位对齐的视觉-触觉路由比完全模态隔离提供了更具可操作性的未来到动作桥梁。

Conclusion: 受控对比表明，在未来的视觉-触觉信息路由中，选择性相位对齐的跨模态访问（而非完全隔离）能为动作专家提供更具可操作性的信息桥梁。AFM的成功验证了当未来信号为理想（GT）时，相位局部化的视觉到触觉信息流动对动作生成具有显著增益。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation remains challenging because successful control depends on physical interaction cues that are often weakly observable from vision alone. Recent tactile world action models jointly model future visual observations and tactile signals to guide action generation, but how such futures should be structured for effective use by the action expert remains underexplored. Directly studying this question with learned world action models is difficult because end-to-end behavior entangles physically invalid visual futures, unreliable predictions, inaccurate or cross-modally inconsistent tactile forecasts, and an unreadable future-to-action interface. To make this interface independently studyable, we introduce Oracle Visuo-Tactile Foresight (OVTF), a controlled framework that supplies paired RGB and tactile futures from successful trajectories verified in simulation. By fixing the future provider, OVTF isolates the interface and asks a cleaner question: if the future is successful and physically executable, what representation allows the action expert to absorb its benefit? Within OVTF, we propose Asymmetric Phase-Local Future Memory (AFM), in which visual memory reads future vision, each tactile memory jointly attends to its own tactile stream and phase-aligned future vision, and cross-tactile access is blocked. We compare AFM with Modality-Isolated Future Memory (IFM), which removes visual-to-tactile access and processes each future modality independently. Across seven tasks on the UniVTAC simulation benchmark, AFM achieves 32.0% average success, compared with 23.7% for IFM and 14.9% for UniVTAC-ACT. This controlled comparison shows that selective phase-aligned visual-tactile routing provides a more actionable future-to-action bridge than complete modality isolation.

</details>


### [76] [DexMani: Human-Derived Manipulability Guidance for Dexterous Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00554)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Shengcheng Luo,Haoran Guo,Jiaming Jiang,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexMani是一个将人类演示作为接触条件可操控性演化先验来引导强化学习的框架，使灵巧手能够在不同机器人灵巧手平台上（Shadow Hand、Allegro Hand、XHand、LEAP Hand）实现物体旋转技能，并在所有评估场景中达到最高成功率（LEAP Hand平均成功率57.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧物体旋转本质上是顺序接触问题，每个支撑、释放和重新接触决策不仅需要产生期望的物体运动，还需要为后续持续旋转准备手部构型。现有强化学习方法在特定机器人灵巧手形态上通过试错发现运动模式，但没有显式考虑每次接触转换如何影响手在后续步骤中维持物体旋转的能力。

Method: DexMani提出将人类演示转换为接触条件可操控性演化（contact-conditioned manipulability evolution）作为先验，该先验捕捉成功的人类接触转换如何重塑手部可用的物体旋转方向。框架学习这种可操控性演化，并利用其引导下游强化学习，从而跨不同运动学和主动接触配置的机器人灵巧手平台获取旋转技能。

Result: 在Shadow Hand、Allegro Hand和XHand上，DexMani在所有评估设置（包括已见和未见物体）中均取得最高成功率。在LEAP Hand上达到平均57.5%的成功率，优于其他基线方法，并产生更平滑的旋转运动。

Conclusion: 通过以人类演示中的接触条件可操控性演化作为引导，DexMani能够有效跨多种机器人灵巧手形态迁移物体旋转技能，显著提升成功率并改善运动质量，证明了该先验在顺序接触问题中的泛化价值。

Abstract: Dexterous object rotation is a sequential contact problem: each support, release, and re-contact decision must both produce the desired object motion, and prepare the hand configuration for continued rotation. Existing reinforcement learning methods discover such movement patterns through trial and error on specific robotic hand embodiments, without explicitly accounting for how each contact transition affects the hand's ability to sustain object rotation in subsequent steps. We introduce DexMani, a framework that transfers human demonstrations as contact-conditioned manipulability evolution. This prior captures how successful human contact transitions reshape the object-rotation directions available to the hand. DexMani then learns this manipulability evolution and uses it to guide downstream reinforcement learning, enabling rotation skills to be acquired across robot embodiments with distinct kinematics and active-contact configurations. Across the Shadow Hand, Allegro Hand, and XHand, DexMani achieves the highest success rates in every evaluated setting for both seen and unseen objects. DexMani reaches an average success rate of 57.5% on LEAP Hand, outperforming other baselines and producing smoother rotatory motions. Project site: https://dexmani.github.io

</details>


### [77] [Latency-Tolerant Cloud-Edge Collaborative Vision-Language-Action Models via Emergent Representational Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00569)
*Daojie Peng,Fulong Ma,Bingtao Wang,Sheng Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CloudEdgeVLA是一种云边协同的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，通过将时间错位问题转化为表征学习问题，让云端的VLA模型编码缓慢变化的任务级特征，轻量级边缘头结合最新可用云特征与当前局部视觉进行闭环控制，在无需阻塞同步的情况下实现高成功率与低延迟响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动机器人上部署十亿参数级VLA策略存在系统冲突：语义推理需要云端GPU算力，而闭环控制必须本地响应以应对网络延迟和抖动。现有分层和异步策略虽提升吞吐量，但慢路径表征仍可能过时到达，或需要显式调度和延迟提示。

Method: 将时间不对齐视为表征学习问题：云端VLA编码延迟观测为缓慢变化的任务特征，轻量级边缘头将最新可用云特征与当前局部视觉结合。训练时，当前帧和随机延迟帧与同一当前动作目标配对成新鲜路径和过时路径，鼓励云端表征保留任务级信息，边缘路径提供状态敏感修正。

Result: 在四个LIBERO基准套件上，CloudEdgeVLA在40步均匀延迟窗口下保持63.8%-78.0%的成功率，而VLASH最高仅6.4%，评估的单路径基线最高仅3.0%。

Conclusion: 通过从控制回路中移除阻塞同步，该设计为可扩展的VLA部署提供了实用路径：云端模型可以持续增长，而边缘计算保持轻量且响应迅速。

Abstract: Deploying billion-parameter Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies on mobile robots creates a systems conflict: semantic reasoning benefits from cloud GPUs, whereas closed-loop control must respond locally despite network delay and jitter. Existing hierarchical and asynchronous policies improve throughput, but their slow-path representations can still arrive stale or require explicit scheduling and delay cues. We introduce CloudEdgeVLA, a cloud-edge policy that treats temporal misalignment as a representation-learning problem. A cloud VLA encodes delayed observations into slowly varying task features, while a lightweight edge head combines the latest available cloud feature with current local vision. During training, current and randomly delayed frames are paired with the same current action target in fresh and stale paths. This objective encourages the cloud representation to preserve task-level information while the edge path supplies state-sensitive corrections. Across four LIBERO suites, CloudEdgeVLA retains 63.8--78.0% success with a 40-step uniform-delay window, whereas VLASH reaches at most 6.4% and the evaluated single-path baselines at most 3.0%. By removing blocking synchronization from the control loop, the design offers a practical route to scalable VLA deployment in which cloud models can grow while edge computation remains lightweight and responsive.

</details>


### [78] [Grasp Execution Without a Planner: Configuration-Space Grasp Distance Fields with Certified Safety & Guaranteed Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00600)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs)，一种在机械臂-手部配置空间上的平滑softmin距离场，通过一个静态反馈律沿负梯度方向执行抓取，替代传统规划器、存储轨迹和抓取选择。通过CBF-CLF二次规划(QP)滤波保证安全，加入磁滞模式切换处理手-物接触切换，并以扳手质量屏障保持力闭合裕度。在固定基座机械臂和Unitree G1人形机器人上，成功从50个物体中抓取并举起46个，保持合成质量裕度的中位数94%，每步QP求解仅0.09ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多指抓取执行架构需先规划无碰撞轨迹再跟踪，但执行时物体位姿不确定性或扰动可能使已规划轨迹失效，导致代价高昂的重新规划。作者旨在消除对规划器、存储轨迹和抓取选择的依赖，实现无需重规划的鲁棒抓取执行。

Method: 1) 构建Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs)：在机械臂-手配置空间上定义抓取候选的softmin平滑距离场；2) 控制器沿该场的负梯度运行静态反馈律；3) 通过CBF-CLF QP滤波，约束自碰撞、工作空间、物体及障碍物间隙，并以显式松弛量报告受阻进展；4) 在预抓取配置处使用带磁滞的模态切换处理手-物接触切换；5) 利用扳手质量屏障将实际抓取的力闭合裕度保持在预设容差内；6) 理论证明softmin以logN/ρ精度逼近真实集合距离，且滤波环路保持安全集前向不变。

Result: 在固定基座机械臂和Unitree G1人形机器人（均配相同欠驱动手）的动力学仿真中，控制器在杂乱和动态场景中成功安全抓取并举起了50个物体中的46个（涵盖基本、家居和对抗性类别）。执行抓取保持中位数94%的合成质量裕度，QP求解时间每20ms控制步仅0.09ms。逐步softmax权重确认控制器执行最近候选，无需单独的抓取选择步骤。

Conclusion: GDFs方法通过平滑距离场和CBF-CLF安全滤波，实现了无需规划器和抓取选择的鲁棒抓取执行，在动态杂乱环境中具有高成功率和计算效率，为抓取执行提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Standard multifingered grasp execution architectures plan a collision-free trajectory to a selected grasp pose and track it with a feedback law. Execution-time object pose uncertainty or perturbations may invalidate the planned trajectory, forcing a costly replanning step. We present Grasp Distance Fields (GDFs), a smooth softmin distance field over grasp configurations in the arm-hand configuration space. Our controller executes a grasp by following this field's negative gradient with a stationary feedback law, eliminating the need for a planner, stored trajectory, or grasp selection. For safety, we filter this command through a CBF-CLF quadratic program (QP) constraining self-collision, workspace, object, and obstacle clearance, reporting impeded progress as explicit slack. We prove that the softmin tracks the true set distance within \(\log N/ρ\) for \(N\) candidates and smoothing parameter \(ρ\), and that the filtered loop renders the safe set forward invariant. Since no smooth field captures hand-object contact switching, we switch modes with hysteresis at a pregrasp configuration, trading object collision avoidance for contact admission. A wrench-quality barrier then keeps the realized grasp's force-closure margin within a prescribed tolerance of its value at hold onset. We evaluate in kinematic simulation on a fixed-base arm and a Unitree G1 humanoid, both fitted with the same underactuated hand. Our controller navigates cluttered and dynamic scenes to safely reach for, grasp, and lift 46 of 50 objects spanning primitive, household, and adversarial classes. Across those lifts, the executed grasps retain a median 94\% of their synthesized quality margin, at 0.09 ms QP solve time per 20 ms control step. Per-step softmax weights confirm that our controller executes the nearest candidate, obviating a separate selection step. Project page: www.clintonenwerem.com/gdf.

</details>


### [79] [From Failures to Supervision: DynamicEnvPlan for Robust Long-Horizon Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00613)
*Hao Yuan,Yuxin Wang,Lei Ji,Zhiwei Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DynamicEnvPlan，一个面向动态物理环境的闭环高层规划框架，通过数据合成（规划、扰动、受控修正模块）将动态执行状态转化为恢复导向的训练轨迹，并用分阶段监督微调让智能体同时从标称执行和扰动恢复轨迹中学习。在104个任务-场景组合上，将基础规划器的成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%，并在全部七项评估指标上取得改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界交互本质上是动态的，环境在任务执行过程中可能发生演化，要求智能体在非平稳条件下进行规划与适应。然而现有具身任务基准将此类动态失败仅视为评估结果，而非可学习的恢复信号，缺乏利用执行中扰动数据来训练智能体进行恢复规划的机制。

Method: DynamicEnvPlan采用闭环框架，包含：(1) 高层的具身任务执行设定（人形智能体、高中层原始技能、结构化语义记忆、可控扰动）；(2) 数据合成流水线由规划、扰动、受控修正三个模块构成，将动态执行状态转化为恢复导向的轨迹；(3) 分阶段监督微调（staged SFT），使规划器同时从标称执行与受扰动后的恢复轨迹中学习。

Result: 在104个任务-场景组合上（涵盖i.i.d.、组合泛化和分布外设置）进行微调与评估，DynamicEnvPlan将基础规划器的成功率从33.3%提升至76.2%，并在全部七项对物理世界交互关键的评估指标（包括安全性和可供性合规）上均取得改善。

Conclusion: 动态环境偏差与执行不确定性可通过将扰动执行状态转化为恢复导向的训练信号来系统性地注入训练过程，从而显著增强具身规划器在非平稳物理世界中的适应与恢复能力。该工作为将失败视为可学习的恢复信号提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Physical-world interaction is inherently dynamic, as environments can evolve during execution, requiring agents to adapt their plans under non-stationary conditions. We study this challenge through long-horizon embodied planning under environment deviations and execution uncertainty. Existing embodied-task benchmarks can expose such failures, but these failures are usually treated as evaluation outcomes instead of learnable signals for training agents to recover. In this work, we introduce DynamicEnvPlan, a closed-loop framework for high-level planning in dynamic environments. It extends embodied task execution with humanoid agents, high-level primitive skills, structured semantic memory, and controllable perturbations. Our data synthesis design consists of planning, perturbation, and guarded correction modules that turn dynamic execution states into recovery-oriented traces. The resulting traces are used for staged supervised fine-tuning, enabling the planner to learn from both nominal execution and perturbed recovery trajectories. Using 104 task-scene combinations spanning i.i.d., compositional generalization, and out-of-distribution settings for fine-tuning and evaluation, DynamicEnvPlan boosts success rate from 33.3% for the base planner to 76.2%, while improving across all seven evaluation metrics critical to physical-world interaction, including safety and affordance compliance.

</details>


### [80] [Learning-Based Motion Planning for Dynamic Environments: From Foundational Algorithms to Emerging Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00625)
*Zongyuan Shen,Shalabh Gupta,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Gao Wang,Rui Cheng,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对2015至2025年间动态环境中的运动规划工作进行系统综述，重点分析基于学习的方法如何扩展、补充或与传统经典规划方法相互交互。文章提出了"学习的角色"分类法，将现有方法分为直接策略学习、学习增强的经典规划、混合规划与训练增强四类，并讨论了关键设计维度及未来开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下的运动规划是机器人学的核心问题，涉及自动驾驶、服务机器人、仓储物流、人机协作等多个领域。近年来学习驱动的方法快速发展，但缺乏一个统一框架系统梳理学习与经典规划方法之间的关系，研究者需要清晰的理解来指导方法选择与未来发展。

Method: 论文采用系统性文献综述方法：首先回顾经典规划方法作为算法基础和参考框架，然后提出"学习的角色"（role-of-learning）分类法，依据学习在规划流程中的参与方式将方法归为四类：直接策略学习、学习增强的经典规划、混合规划以及训练增强方法。每类从问题设定、代表性算法、核心思想、集成机制、优势与局限等维度进行总结，并进一步从观测表示、预测不确定性、交互建模、规划器集成、安全约束和训练策略六个方面进行横向分析。

Result: 论文构建了动态环境运动规划的系统性分类框架，厘清了学习与经典方法之间的互补关系；总结了各类方法在不同场景下的适用性与局限性；识别出塑造学习型运动规划的关键设计因素；指出包括仿真到真实迁移、安全可认证规划、密集人群导航、感知-规划耦合和具身AI在内的主要开放挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述表明，学习与经典规划并非替代关系，而是互补融合的。未来的趋势将朝着安全可认证、具备泛化能力并与感知深度耦合的学习型运动规划方向发展，同时跨学科（如具身AI）的融合将推动该领域进一步演进。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments is a fundamental problem in robotics, aiming to generate safe and efficient paths, trajectories, or control actions in the presence of moving obstacles, uncertain predictions, and multi-agent interactions. It has broad applications in autonomous driving, service robotics, warehouse logistics, human-robot collaboration, crowd navigation, and multi-robot systems. This survey reviews representative works published primarily between 2015 and 2025, with a particular focus on how recent learning-based advances extend, complement, or interact with classical planning foundations. We first revisit classical planning methods as algorithmic foundations and reference frameworks for learning-based extensions. We then propose a role-of-learning taxonomy that categorizes existing methods according to how learning participates in the planning pipeline, including direct policy learning, learning-augmented classical planning, hybrid planning, and training enhancement methods. For each category, we summarize the main problem settings, representative algorithms, key ideas, integration mechanisms, strengths, and limitations. We further analyze how observation representations, prediction uncertainty, interaction modeling, planner integration, safety constraints, and training strategies shape learning-based motion planning in dynamic environments. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions, including sim-to-real gap, safe and certifiable planning, dense crowd navigation, perception-planning coupling, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [81] [FlowPilot: Real-Time World-Action Modeling for Agile UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00635)
*Runqing Wang,Ding Yu,Pengyuan Min,Xinhong Zhang,Wei Xiao,Yu Hu,Jie Chen,Fu Zhang,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPilot是一个紧凑的世界-动作联合模型，通过流匹配同时去噪未来深度观测与可执行轨迹，实现实时机载无人机导航。其采用双流混合Transformer结构耦合视频与动作专家，通过共享注意力使场景预测与轨迹生成相互促进。动作以七阶Bernstein多项式参数化，保证C^2连续参考轨迹。在三层级深度金字塔（高吞吐仿真、逼真仿真、真实机载数据）上训练，闭环保真与物理四旋翼实验中均超越基线，在Jetson Orin NX上端到端耗时<18ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机导航方法中存在两类缺陷：map-then-optimize流水线依赖局部重建，计算开销大且缺乏实时性；端到端策略虽有速度优势但缺少显式场景预测，可解释性和安全性不足。作者旨在设计一种既具备显式未来场景预测能力又能实时输出可执行轨迹的紧凑模型。

Method: FlowPilot包含四个核心设计：(1) 流匹配（flow matching）框架联合去噪未来深度观测与可执行轨迹，使世界预测与动作生成共享同一生成范式；(2) 双流混合Transformer（dual-stream mixture-of-transformers）通过共享注意力耦合视频专家与动作专家，让场景预测和轨迹生成相互提供信息；(3) 动作以degree-7 Bernstein多项式参数化，当前状态约束初始控制点，网络仅预测5个自由控制点，从而获得具有闭式速度、加速度和加加速度的C^2连续参考轨迹；(4) 采用三层级深度金字塔训练策略，分别覆盖高吞吐量仿真、照片写实仿真及真实机载数据，实现sim-to-real的平滑迁移。部署时模型以动作中心模式运行，仅输出轨迹。

Result: 在闭环保真实验中，FlowPilot在障碍物密度增加和指令速度高达8m/s的条件下优于学习和优化基线。在物理四旋翼平台上，完整的感知到动作流水线在Jetson Orin NX上运行时间低于18ms，并在杂乱室内及森林环境中仅依靠机载感知与计算达到5.5m/s的飞行速度。

Conclusion: FlowPilot验证了世界模型与动作策略在流匹配框架下联合学习的可行性：显式未来场景预测与轨迹生成通过共享注意力互惠互利，而Bernstein多项式参数化保证了生成轨迹的动态可行性。其在真实硬件上的低延迟和高速度表明该方案具有实际部署价值，为紧凑型端到端导航模型的实时应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: We present FlowPilot, a compact world-action model for real-time onboard UAV navigation from depth. Unlike map-then-optimize pipelines that require local reconstruction or end-to-end policies that lack explicit scene prediction, FlowPilot jointly denoises future depth observations and executable trajectories with flow matching. A dual-stream mixture-of-transformers couples video and action experts through shared attention, allowing future-scene prediction and trajectory generation to inform each other. At deployment, the model runs action-centrically and outputs only a trajectory. To ensure trackability, actions are parameterized as degree-7 Bernstein polynomials: the current state constrains the initial control points, and the network predicts five free control points, yielding C^2-continuous references with closed-form velocity, acceleration and jerk. FlowPilot is trained on a three-level depth pyramid spanning high-throughput simulation, photorealistic simulation, and real onboard data. In closed-loop simulation, it outperforms learning- and optimization-based baselines under increasing clutter and commanded speeds up to 8m/s. On a physical quadrotor, the full perception-to-action pipeline runs in under 18ms on a Jetson Orin NX and reaches 5.5m/s in cluttered indoor and forest environments using only onboard sensing and computation.

</details>


### [82] [Assistant Placement Aria: A Benchmark for Egocentric Placement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00652)
*Amir Belder,Gonçalo Dias Pais,Refael Vivanti,Omri Carmi,Daniel DeTone,Oren Shrout,Ido Gattegno,Ayellet Tal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Assistant Placement Aria，这是首个面向虚拟放置（VP）任务的基准数据集，涵盖全局、局部及人类中心约束，包含合成和真实室内场景的2D图像、3D点云和文本描述，并针对三个子任务（2D面板放置、坐姿建议、电视放置）进行了标注和基础模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统放置任务通常只关注单一预定义的目标位置，而虚拟放置（VP）需要识别场景中所有合理的候选目标位置，这需要同时进行全局和局部的几何、语义及合理性推理。然而这一具有挑战性的领域缺乏相关基准数据，阻碍了研究进展。

Method: 构建了名为Assistant Placement Aria的基准数据集，包含合成与真实室内场景，并为三个子任务（2D面板放置、坐姿建议、电视放置）提供标注。每个场景包含2D图像、3D点云和场景文本描述，并在该基准上评估了多种基础模型的目标检测与分割能力。

Result: 成功构建了覆盖全局、局部和人类中心约束的VP基准数据集，并完成了对多种基础模型在目标检测和分割任务上的评估。

Conclusion: 通过贡献该基准数据集，旨在推动虚拟放置这一未被充分探索但关键的领域的研究发展。

Abstract: Human assistance in robotics spans around several tasks such as navigation, object manipulation, and placement, where a key challenge is selecting target destinations that align with human intentions or preferences. We focus on this challenge in the context of Virtual Placement (VP), the task of identifying all plausible target locations given scene context and human-centric constraints. This differs from traditional placement tasks that typically focus on a single, predefined target location. The VP problem is complex, as it requires both global and local reasoning about the scene's geometry, semantics, and plausibility. To address this gap, we introduce {\bf Assistant Placement Aria}, the first benchmark to explore diverse aspects of VP, including global, local, and human-centric constraints. It contains both synthetic and real indoor scenes annotated for three tasks: (i)~2D Panel Placement, (ii)~Sitting Suggestion, and (iii)~TV Placement. Each scene includes 2D images, a 3D point cloud, and a textual description of the objects within the scene. By contributing this benchmark, we aim to encourage further research in this underexplored and challenging field that is critically dependent on relevant data. We also evaluate several foundation models for object detection and segmentation on our benchmark.

</details>


### [83] [Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT) for Hip Exoskeletons: Metabolic and Biomechanical Validation of a Simulation-Trained Co-Adaptive Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00715)
*Yifei Yuan,Jakob Wolf,Ghaith Androwis,Xianlian Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次提供了基于仿真的多智能体分层训练（SMAT）策略在真实髋关节外骨骼上的生理学验证：在8名健康受试者上，主动辅助相比被动模式降低净代谢率19.7%（p<0.001），且无需受试者个体化重训即可跨速度和地形泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的控制器可以在纯物理仿真中训练外骨骼助行策略，但极少数考虑人机协同适应（co-adaptation）的控制器通过全身体代谢测量在真实用户上得到验证。由于设备改变关节动力学后穿戴者会重组神经肌肉协调，产生非平稳学习问题，因此亟待对现有方法进行生理学层面的有效性与泛化性验证。

Method: 采用四阶段课程SMAT，依次训练肌肉骨骼人体角色与双侧髋关节外骨骼角色，实现人-机协同适应。将训练好的策略直接部署于髋关节外骨骼上，与8名健康成年人进行实验，采用间接测热法在无外骨骼、被动、主动三种条件下测量代谢成本，并结合生物力学分析（髋关节正功率占比）评估策略表现。

Result: 主动辅助相对被动设备降低净代谢率19.7%（p<0.001）；髋关节正功率占比达0.98，确认各受试者均呈现以正功率为主的辅助模式；策略在多种步行速度与地形上均展现良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 单一仿真训练的SMAT策略无需受试者个体化重训练，即可在真实用户上带来显著的代谢收益，并在训练条件之外保持稳健，证明仿真训练策略具备实际临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Learning-based controllers can deliver exoskeleton assistance after training entirely in physics-based simulation, yet few controllers that address human-device co-adaptation have been validated on real users by whole-body metabolic measurement, the standard benchmark for assistive walking. Co-adaptation is challenging: as the device alters joint dynamics, the wearer reorganizes neuromuscular coordination, producing a non-stationary learning problem. Staged Multi-Agent Training (SMAT), a four-stage curriculum that progressively trains a musculoskeletal human actor and a bilateral hip exoskeleton actor, was introduced and shown to reduce simulated hip-muscle activation and provide positive assistance on hardware. This article provides the first physiological validation of SMAT. The policy was deployed on a hip exoskeleton and tested with eight healthy adults, with metabolic cost measured by indirect calorimetry across no-exoskeleton, passive, and active conditions. Active assistance lowered net metabolic rate by 19.7% relative to the passive device (p < 0.001). Biomechanical analysis confirmed predominantly positive hip mechanical power across all subjects (positive-power ratio 0.98), and the policy generalized across walking speeds and terrains. Together, these results show that a single simulation-trained SMAT policy, deployed without subject-specific retraining, delivers a significant metabolic benefit on real users while remaining robust beyond the conditions it was trained on.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Uncertainty-Aware Simulation-Based Inference for Operations Research with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00019)
*Liang Guo,Lin Shaochong,Shen Zuo-Jun Max,Zhang Kun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的、基于不确定性的推理框架，通过短前瞻模拟量化候选步骤的下游预测不确定性，并利用重要性重采样动态选择更可能产生一致数学模型的中间步骤，以提升大语言模型在运筹学数学建模任务中的可靠性，在多个OR基准上优于标准及低温采样基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在运筹学任务中的部署仍具挑战性，因为正确性依赖于连贯的建模过程而非仅最终答案正确。标准的自回归生成采用短视策略，无法预先判断局部分步方案能否合法扩展为全局一致的优化模型，导致局部看似合理的步骤可能传播为灾难性的下游建模或求解器代码错误。

Method: 提出一种不确定性感知、免训练的推理框架。在参数不变的前提下，利用短前瞻模拟（short lookahead simulations）评估中间候选步骤，量化下游预测不确定性或概率集中度，然后通过重要性重采样动态选择更可能生成连贯数学公式的候选步骤。

Result: 在NL4OPT、MAMO和IndustryOR等多个OR基准上的实证评估表明，该框架一致优于标准和低温采样基线，确立了高效、免训练的可靠OR公式生成范式。

Conclusion: 该工作证明无需更新模型参数，仅通过推理阶段的启发式前瞻评估与重采样即可显著提升LLM在运筹学数学建模中的生成可靠性，为训练免费的可靠OR公式生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) for operations research (OR) tasks remains challenging because correctness depends on a coherent modeling process, not merely a correct final answer. Standard autoregressive generation operates on a myopic policy, which sometimes fails to anticipate whether a partial formulation can be validly extended into a globally consistent optimization model. Consequently, locally plausible steps may propagate into catastrophic downstream formulation or solver code errors. To address this, we propose an uncertainty-aware, training-free inference framework for OR mathematical modeling. Without updating model parameters, our method evaluates intermediate candidate steps using short lookahead simulations to quantify downstream predictive uncertainty or probability concentration. Candidates that demonstrate a higher likelihood of yielding coherent mathematical formulations are then dynamically selected via importance resampling. Empirical evaluations across multiple OR benchmarks (including NL4OPT, MAMO, and IndustryOR) demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms both standard and low-temperature baselines, establishing an efficient, training-free paradigm for reliable OR formulation generation.

</details>


### [85] [Learning Compositional Meta-Routing for Agentic Workflows: An Executable Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00106)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可执行的智能体路由基准测试，以及一个预算感知的元路由器，能够从任务文本中组合异构操作。在保留测试集上达到100%成功率，比静态策略成本低43%，但在词汇迁移挑战集上性能下降，表明词汇泛化是主要限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统不仅需要决定产生什么答案，还需要决定哪些推理和执行操作应在其前面。现有路由工作大多孤立地选择模型端点、检索深度或工具，缺乏能够组合异构操作的可执行基准和预算感知的元路由方法。

Method: 引入包含216个训练、72个开发、108个保留测试和108个锁定词汇迁移挑战任务的基准，涵盖数据分析、冻结语料库研究和文档处理。使用独立的正则化逻辑回归头从词和字符特征预测操作概率，在开发数据上进行温度缩放，并在路线成本和操作次数预算下进行贪心组合。

Result: 保留测试集上，学习策略达到100%成功率（静态和固定工作流为93.5%），成本比静态策略低43%；匹配的一次性路由器仅达56.5%。在未触及的挑战集上，学习成功率降至75.9%（静态路由为93.5%），但仍便宜49%且超过一次性路由34.3个百分点。差距表明词汇泛化而非路线执行是主要限制。

Conclusion: 研究建立了可复现的测试平台和有界的概念验证，而非实时LLM性能的证据。结果揭示词汇泛化是当前方法的主要瓶颈，为未来路由策略改进指明方向。

Abstract: Agentic systems must decide not only what answer to produce, but which reasoning and execution operations should precede it. A controller may answer directly, decompose a request, retrieve evidence, execute code, delegate to a specialist, or verify an intermediate result. Existing routing work largely selects model endpoints, retrieval depth, or tools in isolation. We introduce an executable benchmark and a budget-aware meta-router that composes heterogeneous operations from raw task text. The benchmark contains 216 training, 72 development, 108 held-out test, and 108 locked lexical-shift challenge tasks across data analysis, frozen-corpus research, and document processing. Outcomes are machine checked after operations execute. Independent regularized logistic heads predict operation probabilities from word and character features, are temperature-scaled on development data, and are greedily composed under route-cost and action-count budgets. On the held-out test, the learned policy achieves 100% success versus 93.5% for strong static and fixed workflows, with 43% lower cost than the static policy; a matched learned one-shot router reaches 56.5%. On the untouched challenge split, learned success falls to 75.9% and trails static routing at 93.5%, while remaining 49% cheaper and exceeding one-shot routing by 34.3 points. The gap identifies lexical generalization, rather than route execution, as the principal limitation. These results establish a reproducible testbed and a bounded proof of concept, not evidence of live-LLM performance.

</details>


### [86] [MetaRoute-Bench: Evaluating Meta-Decision Policies for Agentic Workflow Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00107)
*Natan Vidra,Alina Kapanova,Arun Kanhai,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaRoute-Bench，一个开放、可检查的基准框架，用于在共享执行模型下比较agentic系统的元决策策略（如直接回答、分解任务、调用工具、委派专家、验证结果等）。初始基准包含180个合成任务剖面、8种路由策略和30对随机种子，共43,200条执行轨迹。实验显示任务感知的组合策略成功率79.4%，优于静态策略76.7%、一次性任务路由67.4%和直接回答52.9%，但代价是更高的成本和延迟。作者强调主要贡献是可复现的评估方法而非生产有效性的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic系统需要频繁执行元决策（直接回答、分解、调用工具、委托、验证、恢复等），这些决策不仅影响任务成功率，还影响成本和延迟。然而这些决策通常被嵌入编排框架中，仅通过聚合任务准确率进行评估，缺乏对元决策策略的系统性比较方法。

Method: 构建MetaRoute-Bench基准框架：(1) 180个合成任务剖面覆盖数据分析、研究和文档处理三大领域；(2) 8种不同的路由策略；(3) 30对配对随机种子；(4) 采用离线执行模型（seeded offline execution model）而非实时部署来生成43,200条执行轨迹；(5) 采用配对95%置信区间进行统计分析，并进行消融实验（限制路由组合为单一操作、移除验证环节）。

Result: 任务感知的组合策略成功率最高（79.4%），优于工作负载特定的静态策略（76.7%）、一次性任务路由（67.4%）和直接回答（52.9%）。相比静态策略，任务感知策略有2.7个百分点的提升（95% CI±2.0），但平均成本高4.7%、延迟高6.4%。消融实验显示限制路由组合为单一操作和移除验证环节时性能损失最大。

Conclusion: MetaRoute-Bench提供了一个可复现的元决策策略评估方法，揭示了路由策略在成功率、成本和延迟之间的权衡。但由于结果来自离线执行模型而非真实部署，主要贡献是评估方法和路由策略权衡分析，不能作为生产环境有效性的直接证据。作者发布了全部生成任务、策略、轨迹、测试和分析产物以支持实际系统验证。

Abstract: Agentic systems must repeatedly decide whether to answer directly, decompose a task, invoke a tool, execute code, delegate to a specialist, verify an intermediate result, or recover from failure. These meta-decisions affect not only task success but also operating cost and latency, yet they are often embedded inside an orchestration framework and evaluated only through aggregate task accuracy. We present MetaRoute-Bench, an open, inspectable framework for comparing meta-decision policies under a shared execution model. The initial benchmark contains 180 synthetic task profiles spanning data analysis, research, and document processing, eight routing policies, and 30 paired random seeds. Across 43,200 traces, a task-aware compositional policy achieves 79.4% success compared with 76.7% for a strong workload-specific static policy, 67.4% for one-shot task routing, and 52.9% for direct answering. Relative to the static policy, this is a 2.7 percentage-point improvement with paired 95% CI of plus or minus 2.0 points, at 4.7% higher mean cost and 6.4% higher latency. Ablations show the largest losses when route composition is restricted to one operation and when verification is removed. These results are generated by a seeded offline execution model rather than a live deployment; accordingly, the primary contribution is a reproducible evaluation method and an analysis of routing-policy tradeoffs, not evidence of production effectiveness. We release task generation, policies, traces, tests, and analysis artifacts to support live-system validation.

</details>


### [87] [Progressive$^2$: A Teacher-Student Progressive Co-Evolving Knowledge Distillation Method for Substantial Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00129)
*Tiancong Cheng,Ying Zhang,Zhiwen Yu,Yifang Yin,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Progressive²，一种渐进式知识蒸馏框架，通过逐步增强的教师网络和逐步缩小的学生网络的组合，解决教师与学生能力差距过大导致蒸馏性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在模型压缩中广泛应用，但当服务器端模型（教师）与客户端需求（学生）之间存在较大能力差距时，蒸馏性能会显著下降。现有方法未能有效缓解这一问题。

Method: Progressive²采用双通道渐进策略。教师侧：按"从粗糙到精细"的语义递进方式，逐步选择更多层参与蒸馏，形成系统化的学习课程；并设计多特征融合适配器，通过Lipschitz连续性理论框架保证训练稳定性。学生侧：不直接训练极小的模型，而是逐步缩减网络规模，与教师形成迭代协同进化。该框架灵活，教师渐进策略可独立部署。

Result: 教师的渐进策略可独立使用以平衡准确率与训练效率；教师与学生联合整合可进一步提升整体性能。

Conclusion: Progressive²是一种灵活且通用的知识蒸馏框架，通过渐进式课程学习缓解能力差距问题，可适应不同部署需求，兼顾准确率与效率。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) is a widely utilized technique for transferring knowledge from a large model (the teacher) to a smaller model (the student). Owing to its flexibility and broad applicability, KD has been extensively applied in the compression of server-side models to meet the Quality of Service (QoS) requirements of client users. Despite significant advancements, the performance of distillation is substantially compromised when a large disparity exists between the capabilities of the server and the requirements of the client. To alleviate this problem, we propose a novel distillation approach, named Progressive$^2$, which operates through the combination of a progressively stronger teacher and a progressively smaller student. On the side of the teacher, rather than involving all layers simultaneously, we progressively select additional layers for distillation following a raw-to-rich semantic progression, establishing a systematic learning curriculum. Furthermore, we design a teacher-side multi-feature fusion adapter for the teacher to improve training stability, which is theoretically supported by the framework of Lipschitz continuity. On the side of the student, rather than directly training a tiny model, we gradually reduce the size of the network to facilitate an iterative co-evolution with the teacher. Progressive$^2$ serves as a flexible framework; the progressive strategy of the teacher can be deployed independently to achieve an optimal balance between accuracy and training efficiency, while the joint integration of the teacher and the student yields further improvements in overall performance.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking Pretraining for Specialized Design Data: Evidence from the JONES-19 Cultural Design Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00135)
*Alexandros Haridis,Charles Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于JONES-19小规模设计图像数据集，比较了ImageNet预训练（领域通用视觉常识）与从头训练（含多裁剪局部采样增广）两种CNN训练策略的判别性能，发现领域通用先验虽能提升性能，但从头训练结合局部采样可有效弥补差距，挑战了对大规模预训练的依赖，表明精心策划的小型高质量设计数据集可能比大规模数据收集更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计档案以图形格式编码专家知识，为设计驱动的机器学习提供了独特测试平台，但现有工作缺乏对此类专门设计数据集中CNN判别性能的系统评估，尤其是对领域通用预训练与从头学习两种策略的比较。

Method: 基于JONES-19数据集（源自《装饰语法》），评估CNN在两种训练策略下的判别性能：(a) ImageNet预训练以引入领域通用的"视觉常识"；(b) 在JONES-19设计数据上从头训练，并辅以重复局部采样（multi-crop）数据增广。

Result: 领域通用先验能提升判别性能，但从头训练结合multi-crop局部采样可有效恢复这些性能增益；对于高度结构化的设计数据，局部设计驱动的表示足以作为学习基础。

Conclusion: 在专门设计领域中，精心策划并捕获经验与形式设计原则的小型高质量数据集，可能比盲目追求大规模数据收集更有效且更具信息量，挑战了对大规模通用预训练的依赖。

Abstract: Design and architectural archives encode expert human knowledge in graphical formats, providing a critical testbed for design-inspired Machine Learning (ML) challenges absent with typical computer vision benchmarks. Building on JONES-19, a small-size image dataset based on The Grammar of Ornament (London, 1857), we evaluate the discriminative performance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) in two model training strategies: (a) ImageNet pretraining for domain-general "visual common sense," and (b) learning from scratch on the design data in JONES-19. We find that while domain-general priors improve discriminative performance, learning from scratch augmented with repeated local sampling (multi-crop) effectively recovers these gains. For highly structured design data, local design-driven representations provide sufficient foundation for learning, challenging a reliance on massive general-purpose pretraining. These findings suggest that in specialized design domains, careful curation of smaller high-quality datasets that capture empirical and formal design principles may prove more effective and informative on the nature of a particular design domain than prioritizing large-scale data collection.

</details>


### [89] [Leak It: A Probabilistic Approach to Training-Data Extraction from Black-Box Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00144)
*Victor Maricato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判了用聚合ROC-AUC评估语言模型成员推断(MIA)的做法，指出这种指标会被无模型的盲基线混淆。作者提出用概率视角研究基于采样的黑盒训练数据泄漏，并在多个数据集上证明：盲基线已能达到极高AUC（WikiMIA上0.97），而真正的危害发生在聚合指标无法反映的逐文档级别——特定文档的标识符（如邮箱）会被精确提取，且随模型容量增大而加剧，代码域的泄漏远强于散文域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA评估使用聚合ROC-AUC，但该指标被无模型的盲基线混淆，无法反映真实的数据泄漏风险。聚合指标掩盖了真正有害的、逐文档级别的隐私泄露。

Method: 采用概率视角，将N个采样样本视为对输出分布p(.|x)的估计，将泄漏信号建模为该分布的泛函。在WikiMIA、MIMIR(Pile)等数据集上，用盲词袋分类器作基线，评估自浓度、金续文恢复等泄漏信号的增量价值；并设计了逐文档标识符提取测试（真实标识符提取+失配前缀对照），在Pythia系列（410M至6.9B）上按域（代码vs散文）分解风险，同时考察温度、nucleus采样、前缀长度及去重的影响。

Result: WikiMIA上盲词袋分类器AUC达0.97，采样未带来增量；MIMIR上自浓度与金续文恢复的增量AUC的95%置信区间包含零。但在Pythia-6.9B上83/500篇Pile文档的真实标识符被精确提取（16.6%，含邮箱文档中21.3%），且失配前缀对照证实泄漏可归因于特定文档。泄漏随容量从5.6%增至16.6%；代码域泄漏约为散文的3倍（代码中任意续文恢复增益+0.44，散文至多+0.014）。温度和nucleus采样影响甚微，16-token前缀即足够，语料去重无缓解效果。

Conclusion: 隐私审计不应报告单一AUC，而应报告按域分解的逐文档提取结果。研究者发布了leakit工具用于黑盒提取审计。

Abstract: Membership inference (MIA) on language models is usually summarised by an aggregate ROC-AUC, but such evaluations are confounded: model-free blind baselines separate members from non-members from surface text alone. We study black-box, sampling-based training-data leakage through a probabilistic lens, treating N samples from p(.|x) as an estimate of the output distribution and casting leakage signals as functionals of it. We extend the blind-baseline critique into the sampling regime: on WikiMIA a blind bag-of-words classifier reaches AUC 0.97 (TPR 0.90 at 5% FPR) and sampling adds nothing, while on an IID Pile split (MIMIR) neither self-concentration nor gold-continuation recovery significantly beats a blind baseline (incremental AUC 95% CI includes zero). Aggregate metrics hide the real harm. The same sampling verbatim-extracts training data for a tail of documents no blind attack can reach. On Pythia-6.9B, 83 of 500 Pile documents bearing a real identifier (16.6%; 21.3% of those bearing an email address) have that exact identifier reproduced AND not reproduced under a mismatched-prefix control, so each leak is attributable to that document, not to a globally common string. This per-document disclosure is invisible to aggregate AUC and grows with capacity (5.6% to 16.6% from 410M to 6.9B). The risk is uneven: identifier leakage is ~3x stronger in code than prose, though prose stays clearly positive and also grows with capacity (4.0% to 12.1%), while recovery of arbitrary held-out continuations is confined to code (+0.44 member gap on GitHub vs at most +0.014 on prose). Temperature and nucleus sampling matter little, a 16-token prefix suffices, and we detect no reduction from corpus deduplication. Privacy audits should report per-document extraction, decomposed by domain, not a single AUC. We release leakit, a black-box extraction-audit tool.

</details>


### [90] [Response Magnitude as a Dominant Signal for Held-Out CRISPRi Perturbation Effect Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00152)
*Mehrdad Shoeibi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统剖析了CRISPRi扰动转录组效应预测在严格留出目标基因划分下的性能差距问题。研究发现，靶基因的log Anderson-Darling距离可由四个确定性标量函数高度预测，而深度MLP编码器会坍缩至边际训练均值。基于四个幅度标量的线性回归性能远超深度模型，随机森林则更优。在零样本跨细胞系迁移中，幅度预测器正向迁移，而表达预测器效果为负或无法解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测CRISPRi扰动对留出目标基因的转录组效应幅度是单细胞生物学中的重要开放问题。近期研究显示简单基线常超越深度扰动预测器，但该现象的底层机制尚不明确。作者希望在Virtual Cell Challenge基准的严格分割下，识别驱动这一性能差距的特定低维信号，并刻画其跨细胞类型的迁移特性。

Method: 在VCC基准采用严格留出目标基因划分，分析靶标（log Anderson-Darling距离）。识别出四个确定性标量函数作为低维信号；构建并对比深度MLP编码器、四标量线性回归、随机森林等模型；通过预指定对照实验归因幅度增益来源（逐行对齐vs维度增加）；在两个外部CRISPRi筛选上进行零样本迁移评估，并重建靶基因端点指标。

Result: 四个幅度标量即可强预测目标；深度MLP编码器坍缩向边际训练均值且标准补救无效；四标量线性回归超过最强x-only经典模型；随机森林（输入+四标量）大幅超越深度模型。预指定对照表明增益来自逐行对齐而非维度增加。零样本迁移中，幅度预测器正向迁移，表达预测器为负；将幅度暴露给深度编码器可改善迁移但仍不敌四标量线性回归。Anderson-Darling列度量的是全转录组响应广度而非靶基因效应强度。

Conclusion: CRISPRi扰动幅度预测中的深度模型优势实际上是低维标量信号驱动的伪象；评估协议（目标基因划分、度量选择）对结论有决定性影响。Anderson-Darling端点不适合作为靶基因效应强度的代理指标，未来工作应以表达型端点和更合理的效应度量来评估跨屏幕迁移。

Abstract: Predicting the magnitude of a CRISPRi perturbation's transcriptomic effect on held-out target genes is an important open problem in single-cell biology. Recent work has documented that simple baselines often match or exceed deep perturbation predictors on related protocols. We study this phenomenon on the Virtual Cell Challenge (VCC) benchmark under a strict held-out target-gene split, identify the specific low-dimensional signal that drives the gap, and characterize how it transfers across cell types. The target is the log Anderson-Darling distance from non-targeting controls, which is strongly predictable from four deterministic scalar functions of the 2,000-dimensional input. A deep MLP encoder with direct access to the full input collapses toward the marginal training mean, and standard remedies do not close the gap. A linear regression on the four magnitude scalars alone exceeds the strongest x-only classical model, while a Random Forest on the input plus the four scalars substantially outperforms our deep proof-of-concept encoder. Two pre-specified controls attribute the magnitude gain to per-row alignment rather than added dimensionality. Under zero-shot transfer to two external CRISPRi screens evaluated against a target-gene endpoint rebuilt from single-cell data, magnitude-only predictors transfer positively whereas expression-only predictors are negative or unresolved. Exposing magnitude to the deep encoder improves transfer over its expression-only counterpart, yet the encoder does not outperform a four-scalar linear regression on the same features. We also find that the Anderson-Darling column distributed with these screens measures transcriptome-wide response breadth rather than target-gene effect strength, so evaluating transfer against it scores a different outcome.

</details>


### [91] [Inference-Time Policy Alignment for Fair Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00175)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Conor Wallace,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种推理时策略塑形框架，通过乘性调整动作概率，使预训练的深度强化学习策略在无需更新参数的情况下实现基于福利的公平性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习智能体优化标量奖励函数，策略一旦部署往往难以适应新的性能标准（如公平性）。已有方法假设公平性偏好预先已知，且需要基于公平性导向的指标完全重新训练策略，成本高昂且不灵活。受大语言模型推理时对齐的启发，作者希望在推理阶段将预训练策略引导至福利公平目标，避免更新基础策略参数。

Method: 将推理时公平性对齐形式化为策略塑形问题，提出乘性策略塑形框架：利用与动作相关的福利分数调整动作概率，不需要对基础策略做任何修改。该框架通用，可与任意深度RL智能体兼容。

Result: 在多个领域的广泛实验中，推理时策略塑形显著改善了基于福利的公平性目标，同时保持核心任务性能不下降。

Conclusion: 推理时策略塑形为预训练RL策略的公平性对齐提供了一种无需重新训练的高效方案，兼顾了公平性改善与原始任务性能保持，具有通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents achieve strong performance by optimizing scalar reward functions. However, once deployed, the policies of these RL agents are often rigid and costly to adapt to new performance criteria. For instance, an agent trained to maximize expected cumulative reward may not accommodate previously unknown stakeholder preferences. Existing approaches to achieve fairness, a type of preference, in RL typically assume that such preferences are known a priori and require complete retraining of the policy under a fairness-oriented metric. Inspired by inference-time alignment in large language models, we investigate the problem of steering a pretrained RL policy toward welfare-based fairness objectives at inference time without updating the base policy's parameters. We formalize inference-time fairness alignment as a policy shaping problem and propose a multiplicative policy shaping framework that adjusts action probabilities using action-dependent welfare scores, thus requiring no modification to the base policy. Our framework is general and compatible with any deep RL agent. Through extensive experiments across multiple domains, we demonstrate that inference-time policy shaping substantially improves welfare-based fairness objectives while preserving core task performance.

</details>


### [92] [AutoCause: A Python framework that automates expert decisions in environmental time-series causal discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00198)
*Marco Ruiz,Miguel Arana-Catania,David R. Ardila,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoCause是一个开源Python工作流，通过记录每个分析决策、从扩展的因果审计模块派生默认设置并允许领域专家覆盖，将环境时间序列因果发现中的主观选择转化为可审计、可复现的分析流程。该工作流封装了三大家族四种因果发现方法，添加非因果参考模型，并按方法计数支持度对链接进行分级。在145个数据集上的评估显示，多数方法支持的链接在合成基准上比单一方法更精确，但在河流拓扑数据上并非如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境时间序列因果发现中，专家在方法选择、条件独立性检验、滞后范围、样本量充分性、多重检验控制和证据解释等方面的决策因数据集而异，导致生成的因果图无法比较、复现或审计。这种不一致的专家实践阻碍了结果的可信度和可验证性。

Method: AutoCause工作流包含：1) 记录每个分析决策以支持审计；2) 从扩展的因果审计模块中派生默认参数；3) 允许领域专家覆盖默认设置；4) 封装来自三个方法家族的四种已建立的因果发现方法；5) 添加非因果参考模型作为对照；6) 根据支持每个链接的方法数量对链接进行分级。在145个数据集上进行了评估，包括DGP-Atlas、TimeGraph和基于拓扑的CausalRivers参考数据集。

Result: 四种因果发现方法在基准数据集上恢复了参考图的互补部分。在合成基准上，有多数方法支持的链接比单一方法支持的链接更精确，但在河流拓扑数据上这一优势不成立。

Conclusion: AutoCause将不一致的专家实践转化为可审计、可重复的分析流程，但仍将因果解释权保留给分析人员。该工具以开源形式提供于GitHub。

Abstract: Environmental time-series causal discovery requires expert decisions about method choice, conditional-independence tests, lag horizons, sample-size adequacy, multiple-testing control, and evidence interpretation. Applied inconsistently across datasets, these choices yield graphs that cannot be compared, reproduced, or audited. We present AutoCause, an open-source Python workflow that records each decision, derives defaults from an extended causal-audit module, and admits domain-informed overrides. The workflow wraps four established causal-discovery methods from three families, adds non-causal reference models, and grades links by method-count support. On 145 datasets from DGP-Atlas, TimeGraph, and a topology-derived CausalRivers reference, the methods recover complementary parts of the reference graphs. Majority-supported links are more precise than single-method links on the synthetic benchmarks but not against river topology. AutoCause converts inconsistent expert practice into an auditable, repeatable analysis; causal interpretation remains with the analyst. Available at https://github.com/marcoruizrueda/autocause.

</details>


### [93] [A Physics-Chemistry-Informed Neural Network (PCINN) for Real-Time Spatial-ALD Coverage Prediction and Reliable Kinetics Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00212)
*Ning Hu,Chang Liu,Yunlei Jiang,Yuan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG
